Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any math_logic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 132. The projective resolution has length at most 217 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_132(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 132. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^217(S, F) is trivial. QED.
